{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_11","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,260p' experiment_plan.yaml\"","aggregated_output":"# experiment_plan.yaml — EC-HTE internal research plan-to-claim mapping\n# Generated 2026-05-31 | project: ec_hte_codex_a\n\nexperiments:\n- id: plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\n  claim: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\n  description: >\n    CPU-only semi-synthetic MVP for H1. Compare uncorrected, oracle global\n    confusion correction, estimated global confusion correction, and\n    stratified/subgroup correction on the positive-correlation worst-case DGP.\n    This isolates structural global-correction bias from finite-sample confusion\n    estimation noise.\n  registry_id: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\n  role: primary\n  priority: P0\n  gpu_budget_hours: 0\n  deps: []\n  matrix_targets:\n  - target_id: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\n    cells:\n    - row: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\n      col: uncorrected_vs_oracle_global\n    - row: exp_001_bias_bound_mvp\n      col: uncorrected_vs_estimated_global\n  - target_id: T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID\n    cells:\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: uncorrected_gate_rmse\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: oracle_global_gate_rmse\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: estimated_global_gate_rmse\n    - row: positive_corr_high_mis_high_cate\n      col: stratified_gate_rmse\n  operational_criteria:\n    primary_metric: gate_rmse\n    secondary_metrics:\n    - mean_subgroup_cate_bias\n    - max_subgroup_cate_bias\n    minimum_replications: 200\n    success_rule: >\n      In the positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity\n      setting, oracle_global - uncorrected must be greater than 0 on the primary\n      metric gate_rmse, with a bootstrap 95% CI lower bound above 0. Estimated global\n      is reported separately; estimated_global - oracle_global quantifies\n      finite-sample confusion-estimation noise.\n    diagnostic_rule: >\n      mean_subgroup_cate_bias and max_subgroup_cate_bias must be reported for all\n      methods as secondary diagnostics. If gate_rmse supports H1 but either bias\n      diagnostic points in the opposite direction, the result is marked mixed and\n      requires Director review before Stage 1.\n    negative_rule: >\n      If oracle_global is not worse than uncorrected under the positive-correlation\n      worst-case setting, H1 is not supported and the next step is to audit DGP\n      construction, correction algebra, metric definitions, and subgroup weighting\n      before running Stage 1.\n\n- id: plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid\n  claim: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\n  description: >\n    GPU-vectorized DGP grid for H1. Systematically vary misclassification\n    heterogeneity, CATE heterogeneity, subgroup count, and correlation direction;\n    compare oracle global and estimated global correction against uncorrected and\n    stratified/subgroup correction.\n  registry_id: exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\n  role: primary\n  priority: P0\n  gpu_budget_hours: 2\n  deps:\n  - plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\n  matrix_targets:\n  - target_id: C_H1_GLOBAL_CORRECTION_WORSE\n    cells:\n    - row: exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid\n      col: grid_oracle_vs_estimated_mechanism_split\n  - target_id: T_STAGE0_DGP_GRID\n    cells:\n    - row: grid_all_conditions\n      col: bias_bound_auc_or_rank_corr\n    - row: grid_all_conditions\n      col: oracle_estimated_gap\n  operational_criteria:\n    primary_metric: global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative\n    secondary_metrics:\n    - bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman\n    - estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse\n    minimum_replications_per_condition: 200\n    success_rule: >\n      Positive-correlation conditions must have at least a 20 percentage-point\n      higher oracle-global-worse rate than negative-correlation controls, with a\n      bootstrap 95% CI lower bound above 0. The bias-bound diagnostic must rank\n      oracle-global-worse conditions with Spearman rho >= 0.50 and bootstrap 95%\n      CI lower bound above 0. Estimated-global results must be reported separately\n      from oracle global to avoid conflating structural bias with\n      confusion-estimation noise.\n    launch_gate: >\n      This experiment may only run after Director approval and GPU allocation.\n      Before allocation it may only prepare code, configs, dry-runs, and proposal\n      artifacts.\n\n- id: plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation\n  claim: C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET\n  description: >\n    Stage 1 correction ablation comparing global, arm-specific, per-subgroup\n    independent, and hierarchical/partial-pooling correction under matched expert\n    label budgets.\n  role: follow_up\n  priority: P1\n  gpu_budget_hours: 4\n  deps:\n  - plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp\n  - plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid\n  matrix_targets:\n  - target_id: C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET\n    cells:\n    - row: low_budget\n      col: partial_pooling_vs_global\n    - row: low_budget\n      col: partial_pooling_vs_per_subgroup\n  - target_id: T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION\n    cells:\n    - row: budget_low\n      col: global\n    - row: budget_low\n      col: per_subgroup_independent\n    - row: budget_low\n      col: partial_pooling\n    - row: budget_mid\n      col: global\n    - row: budget_mid\n      col: per_subgroup_independent\n    - row: budget_mid\n      col: partial_pooling\n  operational_criteria:\n    primary_metric: gate_rmse\n    secondary_metrics:\n    - pehe\n    - mean_subgroup_cate_bias\n    success_rule: >\n      At low expert-label budget, partial pooling must beat global correction on\n      gate_rmse with bootstrap 95% CI upper bound below 0 for\n      partial_pooling_minus_global. Partial pooling must be non-inferior to\n      per-subgroup independent correction with a 5% relative gate_rmse margin;\n      PEHE and mean subgroup CATE bias are secondary diagnostics.\n\n- id: plan_004_realdata_feasibility\n  claim: C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY\n  description: >\n    Rohingya real-data validation feasibility check. Confirm corpus availability,\n    fields, LLM annotation, expert label source, subgroup variables, split\n    availability, and reproduction permissions before treating real-data\n    validation as an executable result track.\n  role: feasibility\n  priority: P0\n  gpu_budget_hours: 0\n  deps: []\n  matrix_targets:\n  - target_id: C_REALDATA_FEASIBILITY\n    cells:\n    - row: rohingya_corpus\n      col: fields_and_label_sources\n    - row: rohingya_corpus\n      col: expert_split_available\n    - row: rohingya_corpus\n      col: subgroup_variables_available\n  operational_criteria:\n    success_rule: >\n      Feasibility is positive only if the available data include human/expert\n      labels or an auditable gold-standard proxy, LLM annotations or a reproducible\n      annotation path, and subgroup variables sufficient for differential\n      misclassification analysis.\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}
